## Corpus Check
- 4866 files · ~5,833,231 words

## Summary
- 54604 nodes · 173206 edges · 1403 communities (1129 shown, 274 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 21184 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
